|\widetilde{u}|_{\sfH^{k+2}(\Omega)}+|B|_{\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S}}(\Omega)}\leq c\big(|\Gradgrad u|_{\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S}}(\Omega)}+|B|_{\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S}}(\Omega)}\big)\leq c\big(|S|_{\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S}}(\Omega)}+|B|_{\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S}}(\Omega)}\big).

Note that the mapping

\begin{array}[]{ccccc}I_{\pi_{N(\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}\varepsilon)}}&:&\Lin\mcB^{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}}(\Omega)&\longrightarrow&\Lin\pi_{N(\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}\varepsilon)}\mcB^{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}}(\Omega)=\mcH_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t},\Gamma_{\!n},\varepsilon}(\Omega)\\
&&B^{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}}_{\ell}&\longmapsto&\pi_{N(\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}\varepsilon)}B^{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}}_{\ell}\end{array}

is a topological isomorphism
(between finite dimensional spaces and with arbitrary norms). Thus

|B|_{\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S}}(\Omega)}\leq c|B|_{\sfL^{2}_{\mathbb{S}}(\Omega)}\leq c|\pi_{N(\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}\varepsilon)}B|_{\sfL^{2}_{\mathbb{S}}(\Omega)}=c|\pi_{N(\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}\varepsilon)}S|_{\sfL^{2}_{\mathbb{S}}(\Omega)}\leq c|S|_{\sfL^{2}_{\mathbb{S}}(\Omega)}\leq c|S|_{\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S}}(\Omega)}.

Finally, we see

S=\Gradgrad\widetilde{u}+B\in\Gradgrad\sfH^{k+2}_{\Gamma_{\!t}}(\Omega)\dotplus\Lin\mcB^{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}}(\Omega)

and

|\widetilde{u}|_{\sfH^{k+2}(\Omega)}+|B|_{\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S}}(\Omega)}\leq c|S|_{\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S}}(\Omega)}.

The other assertions for

\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\Div,\Omega)
,
\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}(\symRot,\Omega)
,
\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\divDiv,\Omega)
,
and
\sfH^{k+1,k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\divDiv,\Omega)

follow analogously.
∎

Proof of Theorem
3.31
.

For
k=0
and

S\in\mcH_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t},\Gamma_{\!n},\varepsilon}(\Omega)\cap\mcB^{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}}(\Omega)^{\bot_{\sfL^{2}_{\mathbb{S},\varepsilon}(\Omega)}}

we have

\displaystyle 0=\langle S,B^{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}}_{\ell}\rangle_{\sfL^{2}_{\mathbb{S},\varepsilon}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle=\langle\pi_{N(\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}\varepsilon)}S,B^{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}}_{\ell}\rangle_{\sfL^{2}_{\mathbb{S},\varepsilon}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle=\langle S,\pi_{N(\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}\varepsilon)}B^{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}}_{\ell}\rangle_{\sfL^{2}_{\mathbb{S},\varepsilon}(\Omega)}

and hence
S=0
by Theorem
3.28
.
Analogously, we see for

S\in\mcH_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t},\Gamma_{\!n},\varepsilon}(\Omega)\cap\mcB^{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}}(\Omega)^{\bot_{\sfL^{2}_{\mathbb{S}}(\Omega)}}

\displaystyle 0=\langle S,B^{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}}_{\ell}\rangle_{\sfL^{2}_{\mathbb{S}}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle=\langle\pi_{N(\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}})}S,\varepsilon^{-1}B^{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}}_{\ell}\rangle_{\sfL^{2}_{\mathbb{S},\varepsilon}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle=\langle S,\pi_{N(\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}})}\varepsilon^{-1}B^{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}}_{\ell}\rangle_{\sfL^{2}_{\mathbb{S},\varepsilon}(\Omega)}

and thus
S=0
again by Theorem
3.28
.
According to (
14
) we can decompose

\displaystyle N(\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}\varepsilon)

\displaystyle=R(\varepsilon^{-1}\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}})\oplus_{\sfL^{2}_{\mathbb{S},\varepsilon}(\Omega)}\mcH_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t},\Gamma_{\!n},\varepsilon}(\Omega),

\displaystyle N(\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}})

\displaystyle=R(\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Gradgrad}_{\Gamma_{\!t}})\oplus_{\sfL^{2}_{\mathbb{S},\varepsilon}(\Omega)}\mcH_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t},\Gamma_{\!n},\varepsilon}(\Omega),

which shows by (
17
) the other two assertions.
Let
k\geq 0
. The case
k=0
and Theorem
3.16
show

\displaystyle N(\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k}\varepsilon)\cap\mcB^{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}}(\Omega)^{\bot_{\sfL^{2}_{\mathbb{S},\varepsilon}(\Omega)}}

\displaystyle=\varepsilon^{-1}\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\Omega)\cap N(\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}\varepsilon)\cap\mcB^{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}}(\Omega)^{\bot_{\sfL^{2}_{\mathbb{S},\varepsilon}(\Omega)}}

\displaystyle=\varepsilon^{-1}\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\Omega)\cap R(\varepsilon^{-1}\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}})

\displaystyle=R(\varepsilon^{-1}\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k})=\varepsilon^{-1}\symRot\sfH^{k+1}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\Omega),

\displaystyle N(\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k})\cap\mcB^{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}}(\Omega)^{\bot_{\sfL^{2}_{\mathbb{S}}(\Omega)}}

\displaystyle=\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}(\Omega)\cap N(\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}})\cap\mcB^{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}}(\Omega)^{\bot_{\sfL^{2}_{\mathbb{S}}(\Omega)}}

\displaystyle=\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}(\Omega)\cap R(\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Gradgrad}_{\Gamma_{\!t}})

\displaystyle=R(\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Gradgrad}_{\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k})=\Gradgrad\sfH^{k+2}_{\Gamma_{\!t}}(\Omega).

Analogously we prove the assertions for the remaining

\sfL^{2}_{\mathbb{T},\mu}(\Omega)
-related spaces.
∎

Appendix D
Some Technical Remarks

Remark D.1

(bounded regular decompositions from bounded regular potentials)
.

It holds

\displaystyle\Rot\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}=\Rot\mathcal{Q}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}

\displaystyle=\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k},

\displaystyle\Div\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}=\Div\mathcal{Q}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}

\displaystyle=\Div_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k},

\displaystyle\symRot\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}=\symRot\mathcal{Q}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}

\displaystyle=\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k},

\displaystyle\divDiv\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}=\divDiv\mathcal{Q}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}

\displaystyle=\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k},

\displaystyle\divDiv\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k+1,k,1}=\divDiv\mathcal{Q}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k+1,k,1}

\displaystyle=\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k+1,k}.

Therefore, the kernels
\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t},0}(\Rot,\Omega)
,
\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t},0}(\Div,\Omega)
,

\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t},0}(\symRot,\Omega)
,
and
\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t},0}(\divDiv,\Omega)
,
\sfH^{k+1}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t},0}(\divDiv,\Omega)

are invariant under

\mathcal{Q}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
,
\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
,

\mathcal{Q}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
,
\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
,

\mathcal{Q}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
,
\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
,

\mathcal{Q}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
,
\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
,
and
\mathcal{Q}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k+1,k,1}
,
\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k+1,k,1}
,
respectively. Moreover,

\displaystyle R(\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1})

\displaystyle=R(\mathcal{P}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k}),

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{Q}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}(\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k})_{\bot}^{-1}\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k},

\displaystyle R(\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1})

\displaystyle=R(\mathcal{P}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k}),

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{Q}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}(\Div_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k})_{\bot}^{-1}\Div_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k},

\displaystyle R(\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1})

\displaystyle=R(\mathcal{P}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k}),

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{Q}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}(\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k})_{\bot}^{-1}\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k},

\displaystyle R(\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1})

\displaystyle=R(\mathcal{P}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k}),

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{Q}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}(\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k})_{\bot}^{-1}\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k},

\displaystyle R(\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k+1,k,1})

\displaystyle=R(\mathcal{P}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k+1,k}),

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k+1,k,1}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{Q}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k+1,k,1}(\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k+1,k})_{\bot}^{-1}\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k+1,k}.

Hence,
\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
,
\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
,

\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
,
\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
,

\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k+1,k,1}

coincide with
\mathcal{Q}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
,
\mathcal{Q}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
,

\mathcal{Q}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
,
\mathcal{Q}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
,

\mathcal{Q}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k+1,k,1}

on the reduced domains of definition

D((\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k})_{\bot}),\quad D((\Div_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k})_{\bot}),\quad D((\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k})_{\bot}),\quad D((\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k})_{\bot}),\quad D((\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k+1,k})_{\bot}),

respectively. Thus
\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
,
\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
,

\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
,
\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
,

\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k+1,k,1}

may differ from
\mathcal{Q}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
,
\mathcal{Q}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
,

\mathcal{Q}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
,
\mathcal{Q}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
,

\mathcal{Q}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k+1,k,1}

only on the kernels

N(\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k})=\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t},0}(\Rot,\Omega),\;N(\Div_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k})=\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t},0}(\Div,\Omega),\;N(\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k})=\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t},0}(\symRot,\Omega),

and
N(\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k})=\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t},0}(\divDiv,\Omega)
,

N(\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k+1,k})=\sfH^{k+1}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t},0}(\divDiv,\Omega)
,
respectively.

Remark D.2

(projections)
.

Recall Theorem
3.25
, e.g.,
for
\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k}

\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}(\divDiv,\Omega)=R(\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1})\dotplus R(\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k}).

(i)

\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
and

\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k}=1-\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}

are projections.

(i’)

\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k}=\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k}\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}=0
.

(ii)

For
I_{\pm}:=\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}\pm\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k}

it holds
I_{+}=I_{-}^{2}=\id_{\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}(\divDiv,\Omega)}
.
Therefore,
I_{+}
,
I_{-}^{2}
, as well as

I_{-}=2\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}-\id_{\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}(\divDiv,\Omega)}

are topological isomorphisms on
\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}(\divDiv,\Omega)
.

(iii)

There exists
c>0
such that for all
S\in\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}(\divDiv,\Omega)

\displaystyle c|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}S|_{\sfH^{k+2}_{\mathbb{S}}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle\leq|\divDiv S|_{\sfH^{k}(\Omega)}\leq|S|_{\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S}}(\divDiv,\Omega)},

\displaystyle|\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k}S|_{\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S}}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle\leq|S|_{\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S}}(\Omega)}+|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}S|_{\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S}}(\Omega)}.

(iii’)

For
S\in\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t},0}(\divDiv,\Omega)
we have
\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}S=0

and
\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k}S=S
.
In particular,
\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k}
is onto.

Similar results to (i)-(iii’) hold also for

\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k}
,
\Div_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k}
,
\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k}
, and
\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k+1,k}
.
In particular,
\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
,
\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
,

\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
,
\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k+1,k,1}
,
and
\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
,
\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
,

\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
,
\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k+1,k,1}

are projections and there exists
c>0
such that for all

S\in\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}(\Rot,\Omega)
,
T\in\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}(\Div,\Omega)
,

\widehat{T}\in\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}(\symRot,\Omega)
,
and
\widehat{S}\in\sfH^{k+1,k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}(\divDiv,\Omega)

\displaystyle|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}S|_{\sfH^{k+1}_{\mathbb{S}}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle\leq c|\Rot S|_{\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{T}}(\Omega)},

\displaystyle|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}\widehat{T}|_{\sfH^{k+1}_{\mathbb{T}}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle\leq c|\symRot\widehat{T}|_{\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S}}(\Omega)},

\displaystyle|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}T|_{\sfH^{k+1}_{\mathbb{T}}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle\leq c|\Div T|_{\sfH^{k}(\Omega)},

\displaystyle|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k+1,k,1}\widehat{S}|_{\sfH^{k+2}_{\mathbb{S}}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle\leq c|\divDiv\widehat{S}|_{\sfH^{k}(\Omega)}.

Remark D.3

(bounded regular direct decompositions)
.

By Theorem
3.29
we have, e.g.,

\displaystyle\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}(\Rot,\Omega)

\displaystyle=R(\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1})\dotplus\Lin\mcB^{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}}(\Omega)\dotplus\Gradgrad\sfH^{k+2}_{\Gamma_{\!t}}(\Omega)

\displaystyle=\sfH^{k+1}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}(\Omega)+\Gradgrad\sfH^{k+2}_{\Gamma_{\!t}}(\Omega)

with bounded linear regular direct decomposition operators

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}:\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}(\Rot,\Omega)

\displaystyle\to R(\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1})\subset\sfH^{k+1}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}(\Omega),

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,\infty}:\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}(\Rot,\Omega)

\displaystyle\to\Lin\mcB^{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}}(\Omega)\subset\sfH^{\infty}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t},0}(\Rot,\Omega)\subset\sfH^{k+1}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}(\Omega),

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,0}:\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}(\Rot,\Omega)

\displaystyle\to\sfH^{k+2}_{\Gamma_{\!t}}(\Omega)

satisfying

\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}+\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,\infty}+\Gradgrad\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,0}=\id_{\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}(\Rot,\Omega)}
.

A closer inspection of the proof
allows for a more precise description of these bounded decomposition operators.
For this, let
S\in\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}(\Rot,\Omega)
.
According to Theorem
3.25
and
Remark
D.2
we decompose

S=S_{R}+S_{N}:=\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}S+\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k}S\in R(\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1})\dotplus R(\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k})

with

R(\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k})=\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t},0}(\Rot,\Omega)=N(\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k})
.
By Theorem
3.29
we further decompose

\displaystyle\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t},0}(\Rot,\Omega)\ni S_{N}=\Gradgrad\widetilde{u}+B\in\Gradgrad\sfH^{k+2}_{\Gamma_{\!t}}(\Omega)\dotplus\Lin\mcB^{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}}(\Omega).

Then

\pi_{N(\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}\varepsilon)}S_{N}=\pi_{N(\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}\varepsilon)}B\in\mcH_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t},\Gamma_{\!n},\varepsilon}(\Omega)

and thus

B=I_{\pi_{N(\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}\varepsilon)}}^{-1}\pi_{N(\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}\varepsilon)}S_{N}\in\Lin\mcB^{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}}(\Omega).

Therefore,

\displaystyle\widetilde{u}=\mathcal{P}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Gradgrad},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k}\Gradgrad\widetilde{u}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{P}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Gradgrad},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k}(S_{N}-B)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{P}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Gradgrad},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k}(1-I_{\pi_{N(\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}\varepsilon)}}^{-1}\pi_{N(\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}\varepsilon)})S_{N}.

Finally we see

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}=\mathcal{P}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k}\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k}=\mathcal{Q}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}(\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k})_{\bot}^{-1}\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k},

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,\infty}

\displaystyle=I_{\pi_{N(\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}\varepsilon)}}^{-1}\pi_{N(\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}\varepsilon)}\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k},

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,0}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{P}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Gradgrad},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k}(1-I_{\pi_{N(\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}\varepsilon)}}^{-1}\pi_{N(\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}\varepsilon)})\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k}

with

\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k}=1-\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}
.
Analogously, we have for the other spaces

\displaystyle\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\Div,\Omega)

\displaystyle=R(\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k,1})\dotplus\Lin\mcB^{\Div_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}}(\Omega)\dotplus\Rot\sfH^{k+1}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\Omega)

\displaystyle=\sfH^{k+1}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\Omega)+\Rot\sfH^{k+1}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\Omega),

\displaystyle\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}(\symRot,\Omega)

\displaystyle=R(\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1})\dotplus\Lin\mcB^{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}}(\Omega)\dotplus\devGrad\sfH^{k+1}_{\Gamma_{\!t}}(\Omega)

\displaystyle=\sfH^{k+1}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}(\Omega)+\devGrad\sfH^{k+1}_{\Gamma_{\!t}}(\Omega),

\displaystyle\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\divDiv,\Omega)

\displaystyle=R(\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k,1})\dotplus\Lin\mcB^{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}}(\Omega)\dotplus\symRot\sfH^{k+1}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\Omega)

\displaystyle=\sfH^{k+2}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\Omega)+\symRot\sfH^{k+1}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\Omega),

\displaystyle\sfH^{k+1,k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\divDiv,\Omega)

\displaystyle=R(\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k+1,k,1})\dotplus\Lin\mcB^{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}}(\Omega)\dotplus\symRot\sfH^{k+2}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\Omega)

\displaystyle=\sfH^{k+2}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\Omega)+\symRot\sfH^{k+2}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\Omega)

with bounded linear regular direct decomposition operators

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k,1}:\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\Div,\Omega)

\displaystyle\to R(\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k,1})\subset\sfH^{k+1}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\Omega),

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k,\infty}:\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\Div,\Omega)

\displaystyle\to\Lin\mcB^{\Div_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}}(\Omega)\subset\sfH^{\infty}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n},0}(\Div,\Omega)\subset\sfH^{k+1}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\Omega),

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k,0}:\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\Div,\Omega)

\displaystyle\to\sfH^{k+1}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\Omega),

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}:\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}(\symRot,\Omega)

\displaystyle\to R(\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1})\subset\sfH^{k+1}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}(\Omega),

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,\infty}:\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}(\symRot,\Omega)

\displaystyle\to\Lin\mcB^{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}}(\Omega)\subset\sfH^{\infty}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t},0}(\symRot,\Omega)\subset\sfH^{k+1}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}(\Omega),

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,0}:\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}(\symRot,\Omega)

\displaystyle\to\sfH^{k+1}_{\Gamma_{\!t}}(\Omega),

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k,1}:\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\divDiv,\Omega)

\displaystyle\to R(\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k,1})\subset\sfH^{k+2}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\Omega),

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k,\infty}:\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\divDiv,\Omega)

\displaystyle\to\Lin\mcB^{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}}(\Omega)\subset\sfH^{\infty}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n},0}(\divDiv,\Omega)\subset\sfH^{k+2}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\Omega),

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k,0}:\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\divDiv,\Omega)

\displaystyle\to\sfH^{k+1}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\Omega),

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k+1,k,1}:\sfH^{k+1,k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\divDiv,\Omega)

\displaystyle\to R(\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k+1,k,1})\subset\sfH^{k+2}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\Omega),

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k+1,k,\infty}:\sfH^{k+1,k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\divDiv,\Omega)

\displaystyle\to\Lin\mcB^{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}}(\Omega)\subset\sfH^{\infty}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n},0}(\divDiv,\Omega)\subset\sfH^{k+2}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\Omega),

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k+1,k,0}:\sfH^{k+1,k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\divDiv,\Omega)

\displaystyle\to\sfH^{k+2}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\Omega)

satisfying

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k,1}+\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k,\infty}+\Rot\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k,0}

\displaystyle=\id_{\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\Div,\Omega)},

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}+\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,\infty}+\devGrad\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,0}

\displaystyle=\id_{\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}(\symRot,\Omega)},

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k,1}+\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k,\infty}+\symRot\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k,0}

\displaystyle=\id_{\sfH^{k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\divDiv,\Omega)},

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k+1,k,1}+\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k+1,k,\infty}+\symRot\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k+1,k,0}

\displaystyle=\id_{\sfH^{k+1,k}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}(\divDiv,\Omega)}

and

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k,1}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k,1}=\mathcal{P}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k}\Div_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k}=\mathcal{Q}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k,1}(\Div_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k})_{\bot}^{-1}\Div_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k},

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k,\infty}

\displaystyle=I_{\pi_{N(\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}})}}^{-1}\pi_{N(\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}})}\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k},

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k,0}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{P}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k}(1-I_{\pi_{N(\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}})}}^{-1}\pi_{N(\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}})})\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k},

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}=\mathcal{P}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k}\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k}=\mathcal{Q}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1}(\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k})_{\bot}^{-1}\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k},

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,\infty}

\displaystyle=I_{\pi_{N(\Div_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}\mu)}}^{-1}\pi_{N(\Div_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}\mu)}\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k},

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,0}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{P}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Grad},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k}(1-I_{\pi_{N(\Div_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}\mu)}}^{-1}\pi_{N(\Div_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}\mu)})\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k},

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k,1}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k,1}=\mathcal{P}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k}\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k}=\mathcal{Q}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k,1}(\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k})_{\bot}^{-1}\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k},

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k,\infty}

\displaystyle=I_{\pi_{N(\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}})}}^{-1}\pi_{N(\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}})}\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k},

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k,0}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{P}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k}(1-I_{\pi_{N(\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}})}}^{-1}\pi_{N(\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}})})\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k},

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k+1,k,1}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k+1,k,1}=\mathcal{P}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k+1,k}\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k+1,k}=\mathcal{Q}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k+1,k,1}(\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k+1,k})_{\bot}^{-1}\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k+1,k},

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k+1,k,\infty}

\displaystyle=I_{\pi_{N(\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}})}}^{-1}\pi_{N(\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}})}\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k+1,k},

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k+1,k,0}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{P}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k+1}(1-I_{\pi_{N(\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}})}}^{-1}\pi_{N(\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}})})\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k+1,k}

with

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k}

\displaystyle=1-\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\Div_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k,1},

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k}

\displaystyle=1-\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k,1},

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k}

\displaystyle=1-\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T}},\Gamma_{\!t}}^{k,1},

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k+1,k}

\displaystyle=1-\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}_{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S}},\Gamma_{\!n}}^{k+1,k,1},

and

\begin{array}[]{ccccc}I_{\pi_{N(\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}\varepsilon)}}&:&\Lin\mcB^{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}}(\Omega)&\longrightarrow&\Lin\pi_{N(\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}\varepsilon)}\mcB^{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}}(\Omega)=\mcH_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t},\Gamma_{\!n},\varepsilon}(\Omega)\\
&&B^{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}}_{\ell}&\longmapsto&\pi_{N(\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}\varepsilon)}B^{\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}}}_{\ell},\\[12.91663pt]
I_{\pi_{N(\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}})}}&:&\Lin\mcB^{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}}(\Omega)&\longrightarrow&\Lin\pi_{N(\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}})}\varepsilon^{-1}\mcB^{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}}(\Omega)=\mcH_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t},\Gamma_{\!n},\varepsilon}(\Omega)\\
&&B^{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}}_{\ell}&\longmapsto&\pi_{N(\prescript{}{\mathbb{T}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!t}})}\varepsilon^{-1}B^{\divDiv_{\mathbb{S},\Gamma_{\!n}}}_{\ell},\\[12.91663pt]
I_{\pi_{N(\Div_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}\mu)}}&:&\Lin\mcB^{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}}(\Omega)&\longrightarrow&\Lin\pi_{N(\Div_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}\mu)}\mcB^{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}}(\Omega)=\mcH_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t},\Gamma_{\!n},\mu}(\Omega)\\
&&B^{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}}_{\ell}&\longmapsto&\pi_{N(\Div_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}\mu)}B^{\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}}}_{\ell},\\[12.91663pt]
I_{\pi_{N(\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}})}}&:&\Lin\mcB^{\Div_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}}(\Omega)&\longrightarrow&\Lin\pi_{N(\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}})}\mu^{-1}\mcB^{\Div_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}}(\Omega)=\mcH_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t},\Gamma_{\!n},\mu}(\Omega)\\
&&B^{\Div_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}}_{\ell}&\longmapsto&\pi_{N(\prescript{}{\mathbb{S}}{\Rot}_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!t}})}\mu^{-1}B^{\Div_{\mathbb{T},\Gamma_{\!n}}}_{\ell}.\end{array}
